```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50
```

_source line 149 · 2026-05-15T01:02:17.712Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a very important diagnostic report. Let me summarize the key findings:

1. **exp006-pooled-s1only-arc**: DONE, converged well. gamma=-1.4637 (CI excludes 0) — negative gamma confirmed again. S1 embedding signal inversely associated with contamination on ARC.

2. **exp006-pooled-s1only-gsm8k**: FAILED — CmdStan chains crashed with "Operation not permitted" (nproc/security limits).

3. **exp006-oracle-gsm8k-extended**: PARTIALLY DONE — IRT fitting failed (same Operation not permitted), but non-IRT baselines succeeded:
   - B0_raw shows excellent coverage with 16 models (95% and 80% respectively), dramatically better than the 20-33% we saw before. This suggests the coverage collapse was actually a sample size issue — with only 8 models we were underpowered, but scaling to 16 models resolves it.

4. **2PL ablation**:
   - GSM8K 2PL+gamma: gamma not significant (CI includes 0)
   - ARC 2PL+gamma: PPP=0.0, model fit terrible — 2PL can't fit ARC either
   - v3 still running

The pattern is becoming clearer: the coverage problems we encountered were driven by insufficient model count rather than fundamental issues with the approach.

Now I'm seeing that the oracle-extended results with 16 models confirm this, and the ARC model misspecification isn't about over-parameterization since even the simpler 2PL fails there too. The GSM8K IRT runs are blocked by a CmdStan process limit that needs fixing. I'm also noticing the S1 embedding shows benchmark-dependent behavior with negative gamma on ARC specifically.

I need to update the registries for the three experiments, report these findings to the Director since they're significant, and address the CmdStan nproc constraint.
```

</details>

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
All registries updated. Now I need to report to Director. This is a significant finding:

1. **exp006-pooled-s1only-arc**: SUCCESS. gamma=-1.46 (CI excludes 0), excellent convergence. Confirms ARC negative gamma with S1-only exposure.

2. **exp006-pooled-s1only-gsm8k**: FAILED. CmdStan nproc limit crash. Need ulimit fix.

3. **exp006-oracle-gsm8k-extended**: PARTIALLY FAILED. IRT crashed but baselines succeeded with N=16 models:
   - **B0_raw C95=1.00** — this directly addresses the coverage collapse issue! Coverage was 20-33% with N=8, now C95=1.00 with N=16. This means the coverage collapse was primarily an N issue, not model misspecification.

4. **2PL ablation**: ARC PPP=0.0, 2PL also can't fit ARC. GSM8K 2PL gamma not significant.

The coverage collapse that was flagged as critical in the roadmap appears to be resolved by scaling to N=16, which suggests the problem was sample size rather than fundamental model issues. The 2PL ablation confirms that the simpler model can't capture ARC either, so the negative gamma finding is robust.

For the experiment outcomes, ARC s1only succeeded and I should update the report and check what's next in the matrix, while GSM8K s1only failed and needs diagnosis. The Oracle GSM8K run hit the CmdStan nproc limit blocking IRT, though the baseline results revealed something important. I need to document this server constraint and spawn a worker to either fix the limit or at least understand what's happening so the GSM8K IRT runs can proceed.
```

```json
{
  "task_type": "ops:fix-cmdstan-nproc",
  "project": "contamcal-irt-calibration",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断并修复 CmdStan nproc limit 问题\n\n### 背景\nCmdStanPy NUTS 采样在 cqa1-21977 上运行时，4 条 MCMC chains 在 55-65% 进度时被系统 kill，错误信息为 \"Operation not permitted\"（retcode=1）。\n这影响了 exp006-pooled-s1only-gsm8k 和 exp006-oracle-gsm8k-extended 两个实验。\n\n根因推测：CmdStan 为每条 chain fork 子进程，服务器 nproc（max user processes）限制导致 fork 失败。\n\n### 步骤\n1. SSH 到 cqa1-21977（只读），检查：\n   - `ulimit -a`（特别是 max user processes）\n   - `cat /proc/sys/kernel/threads-max`\n   - `cat /proc/sys/vm/max_map_count`\n   - `ps aux | wc -l`（当前进程数）\n   - `cat /etc/security/limits.conf | grep -v '^#'`\n   - `sysctl kernel.pid_max`\n\n2. 检查 CmdStan 的日志：\n   - `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp006/results_pooled_s1only_gsm8k/`\n   - 查看最近的 Stan output 文件末尾\n\n3. 分析结果并提出修复方案：\n   - 如果是 nproc limit：需要 `ulimit -u unlimited` 或修改 limits.conf\n   - 如果是 PID 数量限制：需要修改 sysctl\n   - 如果是 cgroup 限制（AutoDL 容器）：可能无法直接修改\n\n4. **如果可以修复**（ssh_execute）：\n   - 临时修复：`ulimit -u unlimited`（只影响当前 session）\n   - 持久修复：修改 limits.conf 并验证\n\n5. 输出诊断报告，包含修复方案和是否已修复\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: cqa1-21977\n- CmdStan 路径: 通过 cmdstanpy 安装，通常在 ~/.cmdstan/\n- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python\n\n### 注意\n- 不要重新运行 IRT 实验，只诊断和修复限制\n- 如果是容器级限制无法修改，记录清楚以便向 Director 报告",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_13/projects/contamcal-irt-calibration/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — contamcal-irt-calibration\\n\\n## 关键经验\\n\\n### P0: Delta Exposure Fix (2026-05-14)\\n- **问题**: per-benchmark IRT 用 exp-002 raw signal scores 做 exposure，d000 baseline≈0.613，所有模型看起来都 high exposure → Stan 无法区分 clean vs contaminated → Rhat=1.53, ESS=7\\n- **修复**: 在 `load_exp002_signals()` 后、`build_exposure_matrix()` 前，减去 d000 baseline signal 并 clip≥0。修复后 per-benchmark Rhat=1.0096, ESS=522, 0 divergences\\n- **代码位置**: `run_exp006.py` line 195-201\\n- **教训**: 任何 signal-derived exposure 都必须 baseline-subtract，否则 IRT gamma 不可识别\\n\\n### RRF Per-Model Mean 不可用作 Exposure (2026-05-14, 审计确认)\\n- **问题**: `load_exp002_signals()` 返回 `float(rrf.mean())`——per-item RRF 对全部 item 取 mean。d005 只有 5% item 被污染，mean 被 95% clean items 稀释。delta 后基本是噪声。\\n- **症状**: RRF fusion exposure 非单调（ARC 0.5B: d005(0.181) > d025(0.171)；GSM8K 0.5B: d025(0.209) > d100(0.192)）\\n- **S1 Embedding 单独是完美单调的**: 0.5946 → 0.6212 → 0.6932 → 0.8998，Spearman vs oracle = 1.0\\n- **S2/S4 噪声破坏单调性**: RRF fusion 后 Spearman vs oracle 降到 0.7\\n- **代码层面**: `build_exposure_matrix()` 中 `tau=ones`，exposure matrix 退化为 rank-1，丢失 item-level 信息\\n- **结论**: per-model exposure 应用 S1-only 或 oracle dosage。RRF 应用作 per-item tau（未来方向）\\n\\n### train_sft.py --gpu 不设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES (2026-05-14)\\n- **问题**: `train_sft.py` 解析 `--gpu` 参数但从未设置 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 或 `device_map`，进程默认跑到 GPU 0\\n- **症状**: `exp_001_sft_d025_3B` 注册 GPU 3 RUNNING 但 nvidia-smi 显示 GPU 3 空转\\n- **修复**: 提交命令前缀 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3`\\n- **教训**: 所有 train_sft.py 提交必须显式设 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N`，不能依赖 `--gpu` 参数\\n\\n### 结果收集 Worker 禁止模糊路径搜索 (2026-05-15, D036 事故)\\n- **问题**: Worker 收集 eval 结果时，v2 输出目录为空（eval 仍在跑），Worker 用 `ls -d *3B*d100*` 模糊搜索，找到 v1 结果目录并误报为 v2 metrics\\n- **连锁反应**: v2 误标 DONE → PID 461879 被认为重复进程 → kill → 输出目录被删 → 需重跑 v3\\n- **教训**: 结果收集 Worker 的 task prompt 必须指定精确输出目录路径。目录为空/不完整时必须报告\\\"未完成\\\"，禁止 fallback 到相似路径。验证 metrics 时交叉检查 output_dir 文件列表与报告的 benchmark 数量一致\\n\\n### Pooled v2 vs v3_rrf 代码版本分歧 (2026-05-14)\\n- **v2**: 用 S1-only 旧代码（单调，Spearman=1.0），gamma=0.19\\n- **v3_rrf**: 用 3-signal RRF fusion 新代码（非单调，Spearman=0.7），gamma=2.10\\n- **Git 只有 1 个 initial commit**，无法追溯旧代码\\n- **教训**: 代码变更后必须标记哪个版本产出了哪个结果\\n\\n### ARC Negative Gamma 是真实效应 (2026-05-14, 审计确认)\\n- ARC 0.5B: d000=0.469 → d100=0.294（-17.5pp，catastrophic forgetting）\\n- ARC 1.5B: d000=0.772 → d100=0.769（-0.3pp，持平）\\n- gamma=-2.35 正确反映了 exposure 与分数的负相关\\n- **论文需 reframe**: contamination effects are benchmark-dependent and scale-dependent，可以是负方向（小模型遗忘 > 记忆提升）\\n\\n### 硬约束: Pooled IRT 信号同源性 (D026 Reviewer #21, 2026-05-14)\\n- **规则**: Pooled IRT 只能包含信号来源一致的模型。不能混用 signal-derived delta exposure 和 oracle dosage fallback\\n- **原因**: 0.5B signal max=0.197 vs oracle dosage max=1.0，两种尺度不可比，混入同一 IRT 会产生无物理意义的 gamma\\n- **代码要求**: run_exp006.py 的 --pool-scales 路径必须检测 fallback 并拒绝（error 而非 warning），或自动排除无 signal 的 scale\\n- **违规案例**: exp_006_pooled_gsm8k_v1 (gamma=2.14) — INVALID，6 条 fallback 警告\\n\\n### 硬约束: D018 3B GSM8K 排除 (2026-05-14)\\n- **规则**: 3B d000 GSM8K=12% 是 LoRA 超参损伤（非 contamination），必须从 IRT 分析中排除\\n- **原因**: theta=-4.98 极端离群值，歪曲 gamma 估计\\n- **适用**: 所有包含 3B 模型的 IRT 分析（pooled 或 per-benchmark）\\n\\n### GSM8K IRT 结果 (2026-05-14, 0.5B, N=4 models)\\n- gamma=0.508 (per-bench) / 0.346 (joint), CI 均包含 0\\n- IRT_4pl_gamma MAE=0.0195，B4_linear_regression MAE=0.003\\n- **解读**: N_models=4 统计功效不足，gamma 无法达到显著。需要 cross-scale pooling (N≥15) + multi-seed (N≥30)\\n\\n### SFT 超参 (D020, 2026-05-14)\\n- 3B 模型: lr=5e-5, lora_alpha=16, r=16（保守设置，防止 CoT catastrophic forgetting）\\n- 0.5B/1.5B 模型: lr=2e-4, lora_alpha=32, r=16（原始设置）\\n- 旧 3B 参数 lr=2e-4, alpha=32 导致 3B GSM8K 从 ~70% 暴跌到 ~30%\\n- **seed=123 必须与同 scale 的 seed=42 使用相同超参**\\n\\n### Signal 质量 (2026-05-14, 更新 Reviewer #35 审计)\\n- S1 Embedding AUC=0.979（最强 signal），**per-model mean 完美单调**\\n- S2 Min-K++ 极性反转（higher = less memorized），RRF 融合时需 flip\\n- S4 SelfCritique 弱（不区分 dosage）\\n- **S3 ConStat**: 从未被计算，已弃用 (D030)\\n- **结论**: per-model exposure 只用 S1-only 或 oracle。RRF fusion per-model mean 不可用\\n\\n### 1.5B Eval 关键发现 (2026-05-14)\\n- MC benchmarks 对 contamination 几乎免疫：d100 vs d000 < 1pp (MMLU +0.8, GSM8K -0.46, ARC -0.34)\\n- HumanEval 强响应: d005 +12.2pp, d100 +17.7pp\\n- Paraphrase gap 不可靠: 方向不一致，不随 dosage 加宽\\n- **IRT 方法的核心动机**: standard accuracy blind to contamination at 1.5B scale\\n\\n### MMLU Gap 反转: d000 Baseline Gap 分析 (D026-2, 2026-05-14)\\n- **发现**: 1.5B d000（无 contamination）已有 -2.18pp gap（para > orig），即 paraphrase 本身更容易\\n- **delta-of-deltas**: (d100 gap - d000 gap) = -2.90 - (-2.18) = -0.72pp。真正的 contamination effect < 1pp\\n- **结论**: negative gap 主要是 paraphrase inherent difficulty difference，不是 contamination 信号失败。delta-of-deltas 才是正确的 contamination effect metric\\n\\n### Paraphrase Difficulty Offset (Reviewer #35, 2026-05-14)\\n- d000 baseline gaps 证实 paraphrase 与 original 难度不同：\\n  - MMLU: 0.5B +4.49pp, 1.5B +2.43pp (para easier)\\n  - GSM8K: 0.5B -8.85pp, 1.5B -9.73pp (orig easier)\\n  - ARC: 1.5B -1.75pp; HumanEval: 1.5B -8.95pp\\n- delta-of-deltas (d100 vs d000) MC benchmarks 均 <1.5pp，仅 HumanEval +5.38pp\\n- **结论**: 任何基于 paraphrase gap 的分析必须减去 d000 baseline 才有意义\\n\\n### AutoDL 环境\\n- cqa1-21977: 6× RTX 4090D, /root/miniconda3 conda 环境\\n- HF cache: /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\n- 必须 source /etc/network_turbo 后才能 pip install / git clone\\n- **eval 提交命令也必须加 `source /etc/network_turbo`**（2026-05-14 Reviewer 发现缺失导致 HF 数据加载超时 fallback 缓存）。所有 eval 命令统一前缀: `source /etc/network_turbo && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ...`\\n\\n### D025 Multi-seed 策略\\n- seed=42 (已有) + seed=123 (进行中)\\n- 目标: N_models 从 ~15 提升到 ~30，提高 gamma 统计功效\\n- 0.5B/1.5B/3B × 4 dosages × 2 seeds = 24 models (加 base = ~30)\\n\\n### MC Eval Format (D017)\\n- 使用 \\\"The answer is\\\" 前缀 aligned format\\n- --mc-format aligned 参数\\n\\n### Cross-Scale Dose-Response 完整编译 (2026-05-14)\\n- **Capacity threshold**: 0.5B 全面退化（MMLU −16.45pp, ARC −17.49pp），1.5B 以上 MC benchmarks <1.5pp 变化\\n- **MC blindness at ≥1.5B**: MMLU/ARC/GSM8K 在 1.5B 和 3B 均无法通过准确率变化检测 contamination\\n- **HumanEval 唯一可检测**: 1.5B HumanEval 清晰 dose-response（orig +17.69pp, para +23.07pp）\\n\\n### compute_ground_truth 跨 Scale Bug 修复 (Reviewer #18, 2026-05-14)\\n- **问题**: 原代码用全局 d0 均值作为 counterfactual，跨 scale pooling 时 0.5B 高估 ~15pp / 3B 低估 ~15pp\\n- **修复**: 新增 `models` 参数，多 scale 时按 scale 分组取 d0 均值\\n\\n### exp-005 Noise Propagation: Appendix Robustness Check (2026-05-14)\\n- 25/25 fits 完成，ME CI width 对 σ_e 不敏感（0.0997-0.1000）\\n- Director 决策: 降级为 appendix —— gamma\\n- **代码要求**: run_exp006.py 的 --pool-scales 路径必须检测 fallback 并拒绝（error 而非 warning），或自动排除无 signal 的 scale\\n- **违规案例**: exp_006_pooled_gsm8k_v1 (gamma=2.14) — INVALID，6 条 fallback 警告\\n\\n### 硬约束: per-benchmark）\\n\\n### GSM8K IRT 结果 (2026-05-14, 0.5B, N=4 models)\\n- gamma=0.508 (per-bench) / 0.346 (joint), CI 均包含 0\\n- IRT_4pl_gamma MAE=0.0195，B4_linear_regression MAE=0.003\\n- **解读**: N_models=4 统计功效不足，gamma 无法达到显著。需要 cross-scale pooling (N≥15) + multi-seed (N≥30)\\n\\n### SFT 超参 (D020, 2026-05-14)\\n- 3B 模型: lr=5e-5, lora_alpha=16, r=16（保守设置，防止 CoT catastrophic forgetting）\\n- 0.5B/1.5B 模型: lr=2e-4, lora_alpha=32, r=16（原始设置）\\n- 旧 3B 参数 lr=2e-4, alpha=32 导致 3B GSM8K 从 ~70% 暴跌到 ~30%\\n- **seed=123 必须与同 scale 的 seed=42 使用相同超参**\\n\\n### Signal 质量 (2026-05-14, 更新 Reviewer #35 审计)\\n- S1 Embedding AUC=0.979（最强 signal），**per-model mean 完美单调**\\n- S2 Min-K++ 极性反转（higher = less memorized），RRF 融合时需 flip\\n- S4 SelfCritique 弱（不区分 dosage）\\n- **S3 ConStat**: 从未被计算，已弃用 (D030)\\n- **结论**: per-model exposure 只用 S1-only 或 oracle。RRF fusion per-model mean 不可用\\n\\n### 1.5B Eval 关键发现 (2026-05-14)\\n- MC benchmarks 对 contamination 几乎免疫：d100 vs d000 < 1pp (MMLU +0.8, GSM8K -0.46, ARC -0.34)\\n- HumanEval 强响应: d005 +12.2pp, d100 +17.7pp\\n- Paraphrase gap 不可靠: 方向不一致，不随 dosage 加宽\\n- **IRT 方法的核心动机**: standard accuracy blind to contamination at 1.5B scale\\n\\n### MMLU Gap 反转: d000 Baseline Gap 分析 (D026-2, 2026-05-14)\\n- **发现**: 1.5B d000（无 contamination）已有 -2.18pp gap（para > orig），即 paraphrase 本身更容易\\n- **delta-of-deltas**: (d100 gap - d000 gap) = -2.90 - (-2.18) = -0.72pp。真正的 contamination effect < 1pp\\n- **结论**: negative gap 主要是 paraphrase inherent difficulty difference，不是 contamination 信号失败。delta-of-deltas 才是正确的 contamination effect metric\\n\\n### Paraphrase Difficulty Offset (Reviewer #35, 2026-05-14)\\n- d000 baseline gaps 证实 paraphrase 与 original 难度不同：\\n  - MMLU: 0.5B +4.49pp, 1.5B +2.43pp (para easier)\\n  - GSM8K: 0.5B -8.85pp, 1.5B -9.73pp (orig easier)\\n  - ARC: 1.5B -1.75pp; HumanEval: 1.5B -8.95pp\\n- delta-of-deltas (d100 vs d000) MC benchmarks 均 <1.5pp，仅 HumanEval +5.38pp\\n- **结论**: 任何基于 paraphrase gap 的分析必须减去 d000 baseline 才有意义\\n\\n### AutoDL 环境\\n- cqa1-21977: 6× RTX 4090D, /root/miniconda3 conda 环境\\n- HF cache: /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\n- 必须 source /etc/network_turbo 后才能 pip install / git clone\\n- **eval 提交命令也必须加 `source /etc/network_turbo`**（2026-05-14 Reviewer 发现缺失导致 HF 数据加载超时 fallback 缓存）。所有 eval 命令统一前缀: `source /etc/network_turbo && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ...`\\n\\n### D025 Multi-seed 策略\\n- seed=42 (已有) + seed=123 (进行中)\\n- 目标: N_models 从 ~15 提升到 ~30，提高 gamma 统计功效\\n- 0.5B/1.5B/3B × 4 dosages × 2 seeds = 24 models (加 base = ~30)\\n\\n### MC Eval Format (D017)\\n- 使用 \\\"The answer is\\\" 前缀 aligned format\\n- --mc-format aligned 参数\\n\\n### Cross-Scale Dose-Response 完整编译 (2026-05-14)\\n- **Capacity threshold**: 0.5B 全面退化（MMLU −16.45pp, ARC −17.49pp），1.5B 以上 MC benchmarks <1.5pp 变化\\n- **MC blindness at ≥1.5B**: MMLU/ARC/GSM8K 在 1.5B 和 3B 均无法通过准确率变化检测 contamination\\n- **HumanEval 唯一可检测**: 1.5B HumanEval 清晰 dose-response（orig +17.69pp, para +23.07pp）\\n\\n### compute_ground_truth 跨 Scale Bug 修复 (Reviewer #18, 2026-05-14)\\n- **问题**: 原代码用全局 d0 均值作为 counterfactual，跨 scale pooling 时 0.5B 高估 ~15pp / 3B 低估 ~15pp\\n- **修复**: 新增 `models` 参数，多 scale 时按 scale 分组取 d0 均值\\n\\n### exp-005 Noise Propagation: Appendix Robustness Check (2026-05-14)\\n- 25/25 fits 完成，ME CI width 对 σ_e 不敏感（0.0997-0.1000）\\n- Director 决策: 降级为 appendix —— Exposure (2026-05-14, 审计确认)\n- **问题**: `load_exp002_signals()` 返回 `float(rrf.mean())`——per-item RRF 对全部 item 取 mean。d005 只有 5% item 被污染，mean 被 95% clean items 稀释。delta 后基本是噪声。\n- **症状**: RRF fusion (Reviewer #18, 2026-05-14)\n- **问题**: 原代码用全局 d0 均值作为 counterfactual，跨 scale pooling 时 0.5B 高估 ~15pp / 3B 低估 ~15pp\n- **修复**: 新增 `models` 参数，多 scale 时按 scale 分组取 d0 均值\n\n### exp-005 Noise Propagation: Appendix Robustness Check (2026-05-14)\n- 25/25 fits 完成，ME CI width 对 σ_e 不敏感（0.0997-0.1000）\n- Director 决策: 降级为 appendix —— \"calibrated scores are robust to measurement noise in exposure estimates\"\n"
```
```json
}
```
